# Store rename inventory 1: frontend, tests, examples (2026-11-10)

Scope: every `t3toolbox/*.py` outside `backend/` (12 files), all of `tests/` (incl. `tests/backend/`, `tests/oracle_sweeps/`), all of `examples/`. Working tree of 2026-11-10 (HEAD b272e5de). Read-only inventory for the store-rename plan (HANDOFF top block, "THE STORE RENAME"); nothing was edited.

## 0. How to read this

**The rename as applied in the proposals.** Old `up` store (Tucker-factor-shaped, letter U) = new **down** store (letter D). Old `down` store (TT-shaped, letters O or D) = new **up** store (letter U). Physical tuple order is kept, so every `(up, down, left, right)` listing becomes `(down, up, left, right)` and every pair of positional names swaps words, not positions. Mechanical maps used for every proposal:

| old | new | kind |
|---|---|---|
| `up_tucker_cores` / `down_tt_cores` | `down_tucker_cores` / `up_tt_cores` | STORE-WORD, spelling changes (loud) |
| `up_tucker_supercore` / `down_tt_supercore` | `down_tucker_supercore` / `up_tt_supercore` | STORE-WORD, loud |
| `up_ranks` <-> `down_ranks`, `up_mask` <-> `down_mask`, `variations_up_mask` <-> `variations_down_mask`, `up_weights` <-> `down_weights`, `up_weight_supercore` <-> `down_weight_supercore` | swap | STORE-WORD, same spelling, meaning swaps (silent) |
| bare `up` / `down` naming a store (locals, prose, string labels) | swap | STORE-WORD |
| `update='ULRO'`; `'U'`, `'L'`, `'R'`, `'O'` | `('down', 'up', 'left', 'right')`; `'down'`, `'left'`, `'right'`, `'up'` | LETTER-API: **U -> 'down', O -> 'up'** (the initials do NOT carry over) |
| two colours `'UL'` / `'RO'` | `('down', 'left')` / `('right', 'up')` | LETTER-API |
| `nU` / `nD` (also `nO`) / `rL` / `rR` in code, signatures, shape comments, prose names | `n_down` / `n_up` / `r_left` / `r_right` | WIDTH |
| `nU` / `nD`, `nO` inside diagrams and formulas | `nD` / `nU` (letters swap; `rL`, `rR` stay) | CORE-LETTER |
| core letters in diagrams/equations: `U_i` (Tucker store), `O_i` or `D_i` (TT store) | `D_i`, `U_i` (simultaneous swap) | CORE-LETTER |

Index style for widths (`n_down_i`, `r_right(i+1)`), the exact `update` form (tuple / frozenset / `None` = all) and the word tuple order are the plan's; the proposals use tuples in data order. Category codes: SW STORE-WORD, LA LETTER-API, WD WIDTH, AK ATTRIBUTE/KWARG (public spelling, see section 2), CL CORE-LETTER, CLL CORE-LETTER on a private/test local (optional), ADJ ADJECTIVE, TST TEST, UNR UNRELATED. Notes: BACKWARDS = adjective wrong today; CONFLICT = letter conflict (reported, not resolved); EXPECTED MESSAGE = test asserts on text owned by the backend; MSG = error-message text; HAZARD = see section 1.

Method: tokenize-aware regex extraction of every line with an `up`/`down` word component, a width symbol (`nU nD nO rL rR` and derived `_NU`, `nnU`, `rrL`, `nDs`, ...), an `update`/letter-string, a U/O/D core letter, or an orthogonality adjective (2,000 candidate lines); each line classified (overrides by hand for every non-mechanical line, mechanical defaults verified by reading), proposals generated by exact token maps. Scripts: session scratchpad.

### 1.1 Conflicts (reported, not resolved)

1. **The `update` letters invert under the words.** Today `'U'` is the Tucker store (old "up") and `'O'` the TT store. In
   words `'U'` becomes `'down'` and `'O'` becomes `'up'`. A substitution by initials (`U` -> `up`) would be wrong
   everywhere. The trap is visible in `tests/backend/test_padded_frame.py:730`, which pairs `('U', 'up'), ('O', 'down')`
   today. 93 LETTER-API lines, all but 9 in tests.
2. **Load-bearing old U: the corewise frame `(U, G, G, G)`.** Here U is `x`'s own Tucker cores, the T3/T4S letter for
   the Tucker factor, and it sits in the DOWN slot. Under the new letters that slot is D. Sites:
   - `manifold.py` 1547, 1564, 1569, 1580, 1581, 1615, 1630, 1634, 1647
   - `uniform_manifold.py` 1281, 1296, 1308, 1309, 1311, 1349, 1364, 1368
   - `fitting.py` 26, 268, 392
   - `shared_geometry.py` 313
   - `examples/fit_hilbert_from_entries_lbfgs.py:11`
   - about 8 test docstrings or comments (App. A)

   The library has three Tucker letters already: U here and in `manifold.py:1569` (`U, G = x.data`), B in the
   `TuckerTensorTrain` doctests, and `kind == 'U'` / `kU` in the T3 tests. All proposals keep U here and flag it.
3. **T4S letters `(U, O, P, Q)`.** T4S's U (Tucker factor) is our D, and T4S's O (outer TT core) is our U. Sites:
   - `frame_variations_format.py:8` and `:1169`
   - `manifold.py:1342-1343` ("outer-orthogonal")
   - `frame_variations_format.py:118` ("outer-orthogonal")
   - the Section 6.3 substitution `(P, Q, O) -> G` at `manifold.py:1554`, `uniform_manifold.py:1283, 1313`,
     `uniform_tucker_tensor_train.py:546, 610`, `tucker_tensor_train.py:4033`, `tests/test_dispatch.py:409`
   - `(U, P)` / `(U, L)` for the base point: `fitting.py:380`, `shared_geometry.py:45`, several test docstrings

   Proposals keep the cited paper formulas and give a "T4S writes ..." correspondence for the frame tuple.
4. **The sampling/jet code transcribes T4S Alg. letters.** `O` is the TT store, as in `nO` and `p['O']`; U is the
   Tucker store, as in `compute_xi(U, ...)`. Files: `tests/_jet_trs_twins.py`, `tests/test_jet_recurrence.py`, and the
   backend probing/jet modules they mirror. Switching to D/U breaks the 1:1 reading against the paper. Proposals keep
   the letters (CLL, App. A) and rename only the store words and `nO` -> `n_up`.
5. **Letter `D` is already in use.**
   - In the old diagrams D means the TT store: `frame_variations_format.py` 63, 72, 101, 1271, 1305-1322, 1362, and
     the `UU, DD, LL, RR` / `U, L, R, D` locals in `T3Frame.__post_init__` (483-573). Other diagrams of the same
     docstring family use `O` for that store (222, 850, 118, 1372, `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1113, 1125`).
     After the rename D must mean the Tucker store, so every such diagram needs a **simultaneous** U<->D swap
     (plus O -> U). A sequential sed (U->D, then D->U) corrupts them.
   - In the test oracles `D` names dense frames (`_padded_frame_oracles.dense_pass/tangent_basis/dense_project`,
     `test_padded_start.py:479, 566`) or directions (`test_dispatch.py:1152-1305`).
   - `D^-1` appears once as a diagonal (`tests/backend/test_optimizers.py:344`), in an unrelated equation.

   No equation in this partition puts a diagonal D next to frame letters.
6. **Comment letters B and R in the dataclass field comments.** These are at `frame_variations_format.py:156-157` and
   `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:110-111`. B stands for the Tucker store and R for the TT store, and the R
   clashes with R = right cores. The proposals use D and U.
7. **Widths that are not frame widths.**
   - `optimizers.py:176-180` and `tests/test_chunk_size_estimator.py:274-276` name a UT3 point's padded Tucker rank
     `nU`. The proposal is `n`, the UT3 symbol; `n_down` would also work.
   - `rL_0*r_1` at `tucker_tensor_train.py:4264` looks like a typo for `r_0*r_1`.
   - `rL_i*rR_i` at `tucker_tensor_train.py:4590-4591`, and the `rL, n, rR` loops over T3 TT ranks in
     `tests/test_tucker_tensor_train.py`, are T3 bonds. Renaming them is consistent but optional.
8. **ADJECTIVE errors that exist today** (flagged BACKWARDS/WRONG in the main table):
   - `frame_variations_format.py:1371-1372`
   - `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1122, 1125`
   - `tucker_tensor_train.py:615` ("Down TT core unfoldings" is the up unfolding), `:2956` ("right unfolding" is the
     up unfolding), `:2978`, `:3002`, `:3108`
   - `tests/test_frame_variations_format.py:447`
   - `tests/test_tucker_tensor_train.py:3202` ("Tucker up-SVD")

   For the backend inventory: `t3_down_svd_tt_core` (exercised at `tests/backend/test_t3_orthogonalization.py:100`)
   has a "down" adjective on a TT-core SVD.

### 1.2 Hazards for the implementation

- **Seed tags.** The test oracle's copy of `_SEED_TAGS` is at `tests/backend/test_padded_frame.py:91-92`, along with
  the `stream_rows('up', ...)` key at `:136`. The integers stay with the PHYSICAL store (Tucker = 0), so the map
  becomes `{'down': 0, 'up': 1, 'left': 2, 'right': 3}`; the words swap, the integers do not. It must match the
  backend's renamed `_SEED_TAGS` exactly, or the bitwise start tests break. Only consistency is tested: the test
  keeps its own copy and would not catch a slip in both.
- **`update` is a jit static.** It is in `backend.padded_frames._STEP_STATICS` (position 7) and is asserted at
  `tests/test_dispatch.py:2416-2418`. The word form must be hashable: a tuple works, a list does not.
- **Silent swaps that no current test or doctest would catch.**
  - The doctests that print a same-spelling attribute run on frames where the up and down ranks are equal, so a
    missed rename still passes: `frame_variations_format.py:338` and `:1840`, `manifold.py:202`, and
    `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:267` (measured: `(3, 4, 3)` / `(3, 4, 4, 3)` on both stores).
  - Only `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:105` (masks `[2,3,4]` vs `[3,4,5]`) and the uniform test fixtures
    `_UP_R`/`_DOWN_R` (`[2,3,4]` vs `[3,4,5]`) distinguish the stores.
  - The ragged fixtures that do distinguish them: `tests/backend/test_curvature.py:114` (`_TUCKER_SLACK`, down
    `(2,4,2)` vs up `(4,4,4)`), `:2788`, and `tests/test_fitting.py:700`.
  - Worth one deliberate slack-frame test per attribute.
- **Expected-message tests coupled to text the backend owns.** These need the backend's new wording:
  - `tests/backend/test_padded_frame.py:405, 409, 732, 737, 760`
  - `tests/backend/test_uniform_padded_frame.py:303, 320, 518, 551`
  - `tests/backend/test_padded_start.py:277/283, 291/296` (the loops and the regexes)
  - `tests/test_frame_variations_format.py:562`, which asserts `'U.shape[-2]=4'` from the frontend's own message
    at `frame_variations_format.py:556`. It changes only if that local is renamed.
- **Keyword construction of the swapped dataclasses.** `UT3FrameMasks(up_mask=...)`,
  `T3FrameWeights(up_weights=...)` and `dataclasses.replace(..., up_mask=...)` would silently put data in the other
  store. There are no such calls in the library, tests, examples or downstream repos today (grep), but user code may
  have them.
- **Positional pairs that must swap together**, or one side reads the wrong store:
  - helper signatures called by keyword: `tests/backend/test_curvature.py:2761/2788/2793` (`_check(..., up=, down=)`)
  - `test_uniform_frame_variations_format.py:30` (`_make_frame(..., up_r, down_r, ...)`)
  - the `dn` locals that pair with `up`: `test_uniform_frame_variations_format.py:250/292/323`,
    `test_dispatch.py:1680/2840`, `test_weighted.py:211/220`, `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1029`

## 2. (a) Compatibility table: frontend public surface

| class / function | old | new | how it breaks |
|---|---|---|---|
| `T3Frame` (dataclass) | field `up_tucker_cores` | `down_tucker_cores` | loud (AttributeError; also ctor keyword and `dataclasses.replace` key) |
| `T3Frame` | field `down_tt_cores` | `up_tt_cores` | loud |
| `T3Frame` | property `up_ranks` | same spelling, now the TT store (old Tucker meaning is `down_ranks`) | **silent** |
| `T3Frame` | property `down_ranks` | same spelling, now the Tucker store | **silent** |
| `T3Frame` | `.data`, `.structure`, `.variation_shapes` | order unchanged; read `(down, up, left, right)` and `(shape, down_ranks, up_ranks, left_ranks, right_ranks, C)` | none |
| `T3Frame` | `__repr__` `up_ranks=` | `down_ranks=` (same numbers) | text only |
| `T3FrameWeights` (dataclass) | fields `up_weights`, `down_weights` | same spelling, swapped | **silent** (also ctor keywords) |
| `T3FrameWeights` | properties `up_ranks`, `down_ranks` | same spelling, swapped | **silent** |
| `UT3Frame` (dataclass) | fields `up_tucker_supercore`, `down_tt_supercore` | `down_tucker_supercore`, `up_tt_supercore` | loud |
| `UT3Frame` | properties `nU`, `nD`, `rL`, `rR` | `n_down`, `n_up`, `r_left`, `r_right` (note: `nU` -> `n_down`, `nD` -> `n_up`) | loud |
| `UT3Frame` | properties `up_ranks`, `down_ranks` | same spelling, swapped | **silent** |
| `UT3Frame` | `uniform_structure` `(d, N, nU, nD, rL, rR, C)` | order unchanged = `(d, N, n_down, n_up, r_left, r_right, C)` | none |
| `UT3Frame.from_t3frame` | keywords `nU=, nD=, rL=, rR=` | `n_down=, n_up=, r_left=, r_right=` | loud (TypeError) |
| `UT3Frame` | `__repr__` `nU=..., nD=..., rL=..., rR=...` | words | text only (tests assert only the `UT3Frame(shape=` prefix) |
| `UT3FrameMasks` (dataclass) | fields `up_mask`, `down_mask` | same spelling, swapped | **silent** (ctor keywords too) |
| `UT3VariationsMasks` (dataclass) | fields `variations_up_mask`, `variations_down_mask` | same spelling, swapped | **silent** |
| `UT3Variations` | properties `nU`, `nD`, `rL`, `rR`; `up_ranks`, `down_ranks` | words; same-spelling ranks swapped | loud / **silent** |
| `UT3Variations.from_t3variations` | keywords `nU=, nD=, rL=, rR=` | words | loud |
| `UT3Variations` | `__repr__` | words | text only |
| `UT3FrameWeights` (dataclass) | fields `up_weight_supercore`, `down_weight_supercore` | same spelling, swapped | **silent** |
| `UT3FrameWeights` | properties `nU`, `nD`, `rL`, `rR`; `up_ranks`, `down_ranks` | words; swapped | loud / **silent** |
| `UT3FrameWeights.from_t3frameweights` | keywords `nU=, nD=, rL=, rR=` | words | loud |
| `UT3FrameWeights` | `__repr__` | words | text only |
| `UT3Tangent.from_t3tangent` | keywords `nU=, nD=, rL=, rR=` | words | loud |
| `T3Tangent.align_padded`, `UT3Tangent.align_padded` | `update: str = 'ULRO'` (letters `'U','L','R','O'`) | word collection, default all four (e.g. `('down', 'up', 'left', 'right')`) | loud if letters are rejected; the plan should make old letter strings raise |
| `T3Tangent` / `UT3Tangent` | `__repr__` `tucker_ranks=` | label unchanged, now reads `frame.down_ranks` | none |
| `fv_aligned_padded_frame`, `ufv_aligned_padded_frame` | `alternate=` | unchanged (only the docstrings name `'UL'`/`'RO'`/`'ULRO'`) | none |
| all `*_orthogonalize_*`, `*_svd_*` methods, `is_left/right_orthogonal`, `already_left_orthogonal=` | | unchanged | none |
| `T3Frame/T3Variations/T3Tangent/UT3Frame/UT3Variations` `.save` / `.load` | positional | unchanged (section 5) | none |

Frontend calls that forward renamed **backend** keywords (the backend inventory owns these):
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:353, 738, 1375`: the `ufv_conversions.*_to_ut3*` calls with `nU=, nD=, rL=, rR=`.
- `uniform_manifold.py:538`: same, via a `dict`.
- `manifold.py:207`, `uniform_manifold.py:180`: `update=`.

No frontend function takes `up_tucker_cores=`/`down_tt_cores=`/`up_ranks=` keywords. Those keywords are backend-only
(`probing.compute_*`, `ranks.frame_has_minimal_ranks`, `ufv_masking.ufv_make_frame_masks`). The test twin
`tests/_jet_trs_twins.py` mirrors the probing keyword `down_tt_cores`.

## 3. (b) Docstring sentences that state the convention (rewrite, not just token-swap)

Each is quoted as it stands; the main table gives a proposed rewrite line by line.

- `frame_variations_format.py:8` (module): "``T3Frame.data = (up, down, left, right) = (U, O, P, Q)``"
- `frame_variations_format.py:70-78` (`T3Frame`): "The components of T3Frame are the "frame cores": -
  up_tucker_cores = (U0, ..., U(d-1)), elm_shape=(nUi, Ni) - down_tt_cores = (D0, ..., D(d-1)),
  elm_shape=(rLi, nDi, rR(i+1)) ..." and the variation shapes "(nDi, Ni)", "(rLi, nUi, rRi)". The diagrams at
  56-66 and 86-108 must be swapped with them.
- `frame_variations_format.py:116-124`: "up_tucker_cores = (U0,...,U(d-1)), orthogonal: U_ia U_ja = delta_ij -
  down_tt_cores = (O0,...,O(d-1)), outer-orthogonal O_aib O_ajb = delta_ij"; gauge "U_ia V_ja = 0". This should
  become the sentence that states the rule: the down store is down-orthogonal, the up store up-orthogonal.
- `frame_variations_format.py:353-355` (`is_orthogonal`): "- up_tucker U_i (all i): ... - down/outer D_i (all i):
  ..."
- `frame_variations_format.py:661-665` (`random_orthogonal`): "**slack** between the frame's four rank stores ...
  returns ``up_ranks=(4,4,4)`` but ``down_ranks=(2,4,2)``".
- `frame_variations_format.py:1370-1374` (`t3_orthogonal_representations`): "tucker_cores = (U0,U1, U2, U3), up
  orthogonal - down_tt_cores = (O0, O1, O2, O3), down orthogonal". **BACKWARDS today.**
- `frame_variations_format.py:1438-1440` (`T3FrameWeights`): "``up`` (on ``H``'s ``nU`` leg), ``down`` (on
  ``V``'s ``nD`` leg), ``left`` (``H``'s ``rL``), ``right`` (``H``'s ``rR``)".
- `frame_variations_format.py:1572-1575` (`from_t3weights`): "``up = down = tucker_weights`` ... (where the
  complement rank ``nD`` equals the Tucker rank ``nU``".
- `frame_variations_format.py:1627-1629` (`check_fw_pair`): "``up``<->``nU``, ``down``<->``nD``,
  ``left``<->``rL``, ``right``<->``rR``".
- `frame_variations_format.py:1666` (`fv_absorb_weights`), `manifold.py:496-497, 531`,
  `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1499`, `uniform_manifold.py:324-325`: "(``down``->V,
  ``up``/``left``/``right``->H)".
- `frame_variations_format.py:1765, 1805` (`fv_aligned_padded_frame`): "a ``'UL'`` pass (up and left stores) and an
  ``'RO'`` pass (right and down)"; "``False`` runs simultaneous ``'ULRO'`` passes".
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:84` (`UT3Frame`): "four padded supercores (``up_tucker``, ``down_tt``,
  ``left_tt``, ``right_tt``)".
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:110-113` (field comments `B_dxo ...`, `R_dixj ...`).
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1007` (`check_ufv_pair`): "to interface with the frame cores (U, L, R, O)".
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1121-1125` (`ut3_orthogonal_representations`): "tucker_cores = (U0,U1, U2,
  U3), up orthogonal ... outer_tt_cores = (O0, O1, O2, O3), down orthogonal". **BACKWARDS today.**
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1190-1191` (`UT3FrameWeights`): as `T3FrameWeights`.
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1348, 1353-1354` (`from_ut3weights`): "``up = down = tucker`` ... the
  complement rank ``nD`` equals the Tucker rank ``nU`` ... a non-minimal tangent has ``nD < nU``".
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1456-1457` (`check_ufw_pair`): "``up`` <-> ``nU``, ``down`` <-> ``nD`` ...".
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py:1611`: "two-colour ``('UL', 'RO')`` pairs".
- `manifold.py:175, 185-186` (`T3Tangent.align_padded`): "# the stores updated ('U', 'L', 'R', 'O')"; "``'UL'`` /
  ``'RO'`` are the two colours of the loop".
- `manifold.py:685` (`T3Tangent.is_gauged`): "``einsum('...ia,...ja->...ij', U_i, V_i) = 0`` ... (Tucker variations
  ⟂ U)".
- `manifold.py:745, 822, 864, 936` (comments): "T3Frame.data = (up, down, left, right) -- no reorder".
- `manifold.py:1342-1343` (`ManifoldGeometry.frame`): "orthonormal frame (U, O, P, Q)"; "the

- `manifold.py:1548, 1581, 1637-1638, 1647-1648` (`CorewiseGeometry`): "whose down/left/right cores are all the TT
  cores ``G``"; "a slack frame with ``nD != nU``". `uniform_manifold.py:1282, 1309-1313` is the uniform twin and adds
  "``up = down = tucker_edge_mask`` (the U-G Tucker edges) ... all three non-up slots".

  stack_shape)``".

- `safety.py:18` (module): "slack between its four rank stores (``up``/``down``, ``left``/``right``)".
- `tucker_tensor_train.py:615` (`get_minimal_ranks`): "Down TT core unfoldings are full rank: ``ni <= (ri*r(i+1))``".
  This names the old store; it should say up.

Test docstrings that state the convention:
- `tests/test_frame_variations_format.py:21-27`: the structure / shape equations "U_i = (nU_i, N_i) ... D_i = (rL_i,
  nD_i, rR_(i+1))".
- `:447`: "# U: up-orthogonal (all i); D: outer-orthogonal (all i)". **BACKWARDS.**
- `tests/backend/test_padded_frame.py:91, 165-166`.
- `tests/backend/test_uniform_padded_frame.py:124`.
- `tests/backend/_padded_frame_oracles.py:41, 176`.

## 4. (c) Doctests whose output changes

**None, when the renames are applied consistently.** 80 doctest input lines change (the main table rows tagged with
`>>>`/`...`):
- `frame_variations_format.py` (40)
- `uniform_frame_variations_format.py` (37)
- `manifold.py:202`
- `tucker_tensor_train.py:3002, 3108` (comments only)

No expected output contains a store word, a width symbol or a letter string. Checked with `doctest.DocTestFinder` over
all 2,047 frontend examples (0 hits). Outputs that print positional tuples are unchanged because the order is kept:
`T3Frame.structure` (`frame_variations_format.py:151-152`), `variation_shapes` (`:237-241`), `uniform_variation_shapes`
(`uniform_frame_variations_format.py:584-585`). `[True, True, True, True]` at `frame_variations_format.py:1850-1851`
is unchanged under the word loop. Reprs with `nU=`/`up_ranks=` are never printed by a doctest.

The catch: section 1.2 lists the four doctests that would also pass with a **missed** rename (`up_ranks` printed on
frames with equal up/down ranks).

## 5. (d) Persistence

- `T3Frame.save/load` (`frame_variations_format.py:678-690`), `T3Variations.save/load` (`:1107-1119`) and
  `T3Tangent.save/load` (`manifold.py:332-341`) use `common.save_core_families` / `load_core_families`. The keys are
  `'f%d_%d' % (family_index, core_index)`: positional, never a name.
- `UT3Frame.save/load` (`uniform_frame_variations_format.py:432-440`) and `UT3Variations` (`:873-881`) use
  `ufv_operations.ufv_save` / `ufv_load`. These are also positional, over the supercores, the masks and the shape
  ints.
- `T3FrameWeights` / `UT3FrameWeights` have no save.
- Because `T3Frame.data` keeps its physical order, a 2026.4.0 file loads in 2026.5.0 into the same physical stores:
  family 0 is the Tucker store, now named `down`. **No format change and no migration.** Only the names used to
  describe family 0/1 flip.
- Tests round-trip only, with no stored fixtures (`tests/test_frame_variations_format.py:353-360`,
  `tests/test_uniform_frame_variations_format.py:738-775`, `tests/test_manifold.py:160-165`).
- No `.npz` lives in `tests/` or `examples/`. `tests/backend/_padded_frame_cycle_case.py` stores a T3 point and a hint
  as literals, with no frame and no store names.
- `T3Polynomial/data/**.npz`: no `savez` call there writes store-named keys (grep).

## 6. (e) Downstream uses of the public spellings (read-only grep)

- **T3Polynomial:**
  - None of the public spellings (`up_tucker_cores`, `down_tt_cores`, `*_ranks`, `*_mask`, `*_weights`,
    `*_supercore`, `.nU/.nD/.rL/.rR`, `nU=`, `update=`).
  - One positional unpack, `up, down, left, right = frame` at `scripts/g01_g0_common.py:121`, in `zero_base`. It
    behaves correctly after the rename (positional), but the names read backwards. Rename to `down, up, left, right`.
  - `t3polynomial/` package code touches frames only through `geom.frame(...)`, `lm.frame` and `frame.data`
    positions.
- **implicit_probing:** does not import `t3toolbox`, so it has no uses.
- **nicks_research_experiments:**
  - No call into the library uses a public store spelling, a width keyword or `update=`.
  - Its own prototypes mirror the OLD convention:
    - `nU, nD, rL, rR = stores` in about 8 files under `tensor/aligned_padded_frame/` and
      `tensor/padded_frame_vs_tree_completion/`
    - `up, down, left, right = frame` in about 10 places
    - their own `update='ULRO'` / `'UL'`/`'RO'` schedules (`cycle/core_form3.align_stores`, `ragged_loop.py`,
      `uniform_loop.py`, `uniform_form2.py`, `cycle/*_schemes.py`, `check_uniform_two_colour.py`)
    - a docstring "LIBRARY FRAME FORMAT: frame = (U, O, L, R, shape, (mU, mD, mL, mR))" at
      `tensor/aligned_padded_frame/uniform_form2.py:16`, which describes the library.
  - Nothing breaks. The prototypes will read against the library's new words. `tensor/legacy_tt_probing/*`'s
    `rL, rR` and `ice_hessian/*`'s `nU` are unrelated.

Store letters in test-local names (`U, O, L, R = frame`, `mU`/`mD`, `pU`/`pD`, `Uu`/`Ou`, `p['O']`, `UU`/`OO`) and docstring letters such as `(U, L)` for a base point.
